{"forms":[{"form":"crashes out","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"crashing out","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"crashed out","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"crashed out","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He crashed out after a long day of work.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fall asleep from exhaustion."],"synonyms":[{"word":"crash"}],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Harry didn't know why but the computer crashed out all of a sudden.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To terminate with an unhandled error; to crash."],"tags":["informal"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He crashed out at the second round of the tournament.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be eliminated from a competition."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["To produce or create rapidly; to bang out."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To rapidly precipitate."],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Losing six games in a row was enough to make my friend start crashing out.","type":"example"},{"text":"If I had a boss like that, I'd crash out too.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To become uncontrollably angry or upset; to rage, snap."],"synonyms":[{"word":"lose one's cool"},{"word":"fly off the handle"},{"word":"go apeshit"},{"word":"throw a fit"},{"word":"erupt"},{"word":"snap"}],"tags":["slang"]},{"glosses":["To break out of a prison."],"tags":["US","slang"]},{"glosses":["To act out recklessly and violently, often in a way that leads to self-destruction or serious consequences, typically in enraged reaction to something."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["Internet"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":3208926,"upstream_offset":8692955802,"upstream_record_sha256":"5cbc5b80d943899d4f257fe988135d382002872fd9db1efe702f9c1f0e900d3d","word":"crash out"}
